  Need remain |s|≤1.

- Lag 0: c0 = ∑ h_i(1-h_i) = n/2 - ∑h_i². To be ≤ target*n/2? Actually C=T => c_max ≤ T*n/2. For T=0.38, n/2=47.5 => c0≤18.05. Thus ∑h_i² ≥ n/2 -18.05. For n=95, csum=47.5, target cmax=18.054. ∑h²≥29.446. Average h²≥0.310; since mean0.5, variance≥? uniform h² sum=23.75, so h needs structure.

Let's design a time-aware multi-phase algorithm:

Phase 0: Establish best from parent and simple deterministic starts (tent, cos, two-block, random smooth, etc) at multiple n? Evaluate only, no optimization. Maybe a deterministic optimized via quick local.

Phase 1: Refine parent at its n using a powerful local sequence:
   - Softmax SLSQP with multiple beta and many iterations, as previous.
   - Custom projected gradient with adaptive beta and hard active set for further tiny improvements.
   - Coordinate/binary tweak if h near binary?

Phase 2: Global search at a few low/medium n:
   - For n in [96,128,160], generate K random smooth feasible starts (AR(1), sine mixtures, random then lowpass), plus binary block starts, plus interpolated parent.
   - For each start, run quick projected gradient (e.g., 200-500 it) to local refine. Keep best.
   - Run DE on n=80 or 96 to find a good h, then refine.
   - Maybe run basin hopping: iteratively perturb best and local refine.

Phase 3: Up-sample best candidate to higher n (e.g., 190, 285) and local refine, because higher resolution often lowers discrete C. Keep best.

Phase 4: Final SLSQP hard on best at its n, plus evaluator call.

 h=np.clip(h,0,1)

Project_h exact sum:
```
def project_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n: h = np.resize(h,n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    # bisection for lambda
    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        mask = (h > 1e-13) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-13)
        if mask.sum() > 0:
            # distribute clipped
            adj = -diff / mask.sum()
            # ensure no violation by iterative small
            for _ in range(20):
                h2 = h.copy()
                h2[mask] = np.clip(h2[mask] + adj, 0.0, 1.0)
                newdiff = target - float(np.sum(h2))
                if abs(newdiff) < 1e-14:
                    h = h2; break
                diff = newdiff
                adj = -diff / mask.sum()
            else:
                h=h2
        else:
            # if all 0/1 and sum off, adjust states? not possible if target m not int; but for odd target .5, we can set one 0.5 if not
            if abs(target - float(np.sum(h))) > 1e-12:
                # find a 0 or1 to move
                idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
                h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + (target - float(np.sum(h))) / 1.0 if False else h[idx] + (target - float(np.sum(h))),0,1))
    return h
```
For even target integer, if all 0/1 and sum exact no issue. For odd target .5, need at least one 0.5. Project may fail if all integers sum not .5. We can explicitly handle: after projection, if target - sum diff and no interior, set one element to 0.5 and adjust others? Let's make robust with iterative water-fill earlier maybe simpler.

def project_h(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1); target=n/2.0
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=h.sum()-target
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:

   if mask.any(): h[mask]-=diff/mask.sum(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```

```
grad=np.zeros(n)
for idx in range(2*n-1):
 wk=weights[idx]
 if wk <= 1e-15: continue
 lag=idx-(n-1)
 if lag >=0:
   m=n-lag
   grad[lag:] += wk*(1-h[:m])
   if m>0: grad[:m] -= wk*h[lag:]

   d=-lag

def slsqp_refine_one(h,n,beta,stop):
 bounds=[(0,1)]*n
 cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
 maxiter = 3000 if n<120 else 1500 if n<250 else 800
 # respect time: SLSQP has no callback; set maxiter conservative.
 try:
  res=minimize(fun=obj_soft, x0=h, jac=obj_soft_grad, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-13})
  h2=project_h(res.x,n); c2=c5...
 except: return h,best
```

```
def random_smooth_h(rng,n):
   # generate low-passed normal field
   x=np.arange(n)
   f=np.ones(n); f[1:]=1.0/np.arange(1,n)  # low pass
   p=rng.normal(size=2*n) + 1j*rng.normal(size=2*n)
   # Use rfft? Simpler real-space convolution with exponential kernel
   kernel_len=max(5,n//5)
   kernel=np.exp(-np.abs(np.arange(-kernel_len,kernel_len+1))/(n/10.0))
   s=np.convolve(rng.normal(size=n), kernel, mode='same')
   s-=s.mean(); s/= (np.std(s)+1e-8)
   s=np.tanh(0.8*s)
   h=0.5+0.5*s
   return project_h(h,n)
```

Random smooth: Need deterministic seed. Use `np.random.default_rng(seed)`? Allowed numpy. To avoid closures, function takes rng.
```
def random_smooth_h(rng,n, scale=0.8):
   s = rng.normal(size=n)
   # lowpass by repeated smoothing
   klen = max(3, n//8)
   kernel = np.exp(-np.square(np.arange(-klen,klen+1)/(2.0*max(1.0,n/20.0))))
   kernel /= kernel.sum()
   s = np.convolve(s, kernel, mode='same')
   s = np.tanh(s / (np.std(s)+1e-8) * scale)
   h=0.5+0.5*s
   return project_h(h,n)
```
This generates smooth zero-ish fields. Scale 0.8 amplitude.

```

Detailed helper implementations:

`project_h`:
```
def project_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(80):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = s - target
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h > 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            h[mask] -= diff / float(mask.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            h[mask] -= diff / float(mask.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        # distribute to entries with max room
        for _ in range(50):
            if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
                room = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if room <= 0: break
                h[idx] += room
                diff -= room
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                room = min(-diff, h[idx])
                if room <= 0: break
                h[idx] -= room
                diff += room
    return h
```
This ensures sum near exact. If target odd .5 and h all 0/1, diff .5; first argmax room maybe 1 if zero? If sum too low diff positive, choose zero (room1) set 0.5. Good.

`c_full`, `c5`:
```
def c_full(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(c_full(h,n)) * (2.0/n))
```
Recomputes c. For value functions, separate.

`grad_w` as above. Ensure weights length 2n-1; if not? Use.

```
def grad_w(weights, h, n):
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    for idx in range(w.size):
        wk = float(w[idx])
        if wk <= 1e-16:
            continue
        lag = idx - (n - 1)
        if lag >= 0:
            m = n - lag
            grad[lag:] += wk * (1.0 - h[:m])
            if m > 0:
                grad[:m] -= wk * h[lag:]
        else:
            d = -lag
            m = n - d
            if m > 0:
                grad[:m] += wk * (1.0 - h[d:])
                if d < n:
                    grad[d:] -= wk * h[:m]
    return grad
```
For lag positive, m=n-lag, h[lag:] length m. For negative, d>0, h[d:] length m, h[:m] length m.

`lag_grad` can call grad_w with `w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[idx]=1.0` but that creates each constraint. We can implement direct:
```
def lag_grad(h,n,idx):
  grad=zeros; lag=idx-(n-1); ... same as one weight
  return grad
```
To avoid duplicate, in NLP use `grad_w` with a global one-hot? But constraints different idx. Direct fine.

```
def lag_grad(h,n,idx):
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    lag=idx-(n-1)
    if lag >=0:
       m=n-lag
       grad[lag:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
       if m>0: grad[:m] -= h[lag:]
    else:
       d=-lag; m=n-d
       if m>0:
          grad[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
          if d<n: grad[d:] -= h[:m]
    return grad
```
Check lag=0: grad += q; grad -= h => 1-2h.

Soft values:
```
def soft_value(h,n,beta):
    c=c_full(h,n); dx=2.0/n
    s=beta*c; m=float(np.max(s))
    e=np.exp(s-m); z=float(np.sum(e))
    if z <= 0.0: return 0.0
    return float((m + np.log(z)) * dx / beta)
def soft_value_grad(h,n,beta):
    c=c_full(h,n); dx=2.0/n
    s=beta*c; m=float(np.max(s))
    e=np.exp(s-m); z=sum; weights=e/z
    val=(m+np.log(z))*dx/beta
    grad=grad_w(weights,h,n)*dx
    return float(val), grad
```
For beta large and all e underflow? At max one e=1, z≥1.

Hard value/grad:
```
def hard_value(h,n): return c5
def hard_grad(h,n, tol=1e-12):
    c=c_full; dx=2/n; cmax=max; active = c >= cmax - max(tol, 1e-8*cmax)
    w=active.astype(float)
    # If none, one-hot
    if not any: w[argmax]=1
    # normalize? for subgradient of max, average active weights sum1. Use divide sum.
    w /= sum(w)
    return grad_w(w,h,n)*dx, float(cmax*dx)
```
Use in PG hard maybe with smoothed weights:
```
def weighted_grad_active(h,n,temp=1e-3):
 c=c_full; cmax=max; w=np.exp((c-cmax)/temp); w/=sum; return grad_w...
```
`temp` in c units. c values up to 50; temp 0.5 maybe includes near max. We'll use hard PG with temp 0.1-0.5? Maybe.

`projected_gradient_refine`:
Need avoid using `soft_value` for trial too expensive? ok.
```
def projected_gradient_refine(h,n,beta,stop,max_iter=500,lr0=0.05):
    h=project_h(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5(h,n)
    F,grad=soft_value_grad(h,n,beta)
    alpha=lr0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > stop: break
        grad = grad - np.mean(grad)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
             break
        step = min(alpha, 0.2 / gnorm)
        improved = False
        for _ in range(25):
            h2 = project_h(h - step * grad, n)
            # If trial too similar skip? 
            F2 = soft_value(h2,n,beta)
            if F2 < F - 1e-15:
                h = h2; F = F2; improved = True; break
            step *= 0.5
        if not improved:
            # try tiny hard-improvement steps along projected gradient
            for tiny in [0.01/gnorm, 0.005/gnorm, 0.002/gnorm]:
                h2 = project_h(h - tiny * grad, n)
                c2 = c5(h2,n)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                    h = h2; best_c = c2; best_h = h.copy()
                    break
            break
        c2 = c5(h,n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2; best_h = h.copy()
        if best_c <= 0.0:
            break
        # recompute F? We have F for new h.
        # adapt alpha
        if F2 < F - 1e-12:
            alpha = min(0.5, alpha * 1.1)
        else:
            alpha *= 0.8
        # early stop if little hard progress over last 50? keep simple
    # final hard recompute
    best_c = min(best_c, c5(best_h,n))
    return best_h, best_c
```
Potential issue: F2 variable referenced outside if improved? It is set in loop; if improved true, yes. But if improved true from first eval, F2 defined. If not, not used. OK.
But `F` should be recomputed after h update; we set F=F2. Good.
Line search uses `step = min(alpha, 0.2/gnorm)`. grad magnitude for soft with beta small maybe ~0.05? alpha 0.05, step maybe0.05. If gnorm=10, step0.02. OK.
Could project many times; project_h loop 80*n, maybe expensive inside line search (25*500). For n=100, 1.2M operations, fine. But for high n and many, project_h 80 loops may be heavy. Optimize project_h using bisection instead? Our iterative waterfill up to 80, but usually converges <5. Final loops okay. Could reduce to 30.
Use 50.

`projected_gradient_hard_active`:
```
def projected_gradient_hard(h,n,stop,max_iter=500,temp=0.05):
    h=project_h(h,n); best...
    for it...
       c=c_full; cmax=max
       w=np.exp((c-cmax)/temp); w/=w.sum()
       grad=grad_w(w,h,n)*(2.0/n); grad-=mean
       gnorm=norm
       step=0.05/gnorm
       improved=False
       for _ in range(20):
          h2=project_h(h-step*grad,n)
          c2=float(np.max(c_full(h2,n))*2/n)
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
             h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if not improved: break
    return best
```

`slsqp_soft(h,n,beta,stop,maxiter=None)`:
```
def slsqp_soft(h,n,beta,stop,maxiter=None):
    best_h=project_h(h,n); best_c=c5(best_h,n)
    if time.time() > stop - 15: return best_h,best_c
    if maxiter is None:
       if n < 120: maxiter=700
       elif n < 250: maxiter=350
       else: maxiter=200
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res = minimize(fun=obj_soft, x0=best_h, jac=obj_soft_grad, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13})
       h2=project_h(res.x,n); c2=c5(h2,n)
       if c2<best_c: return h2,c2
    except Exception: pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
`obj_soft` top-level:
```
def obj_soft(h,n,beta): return soft_value(h,n,beta)
def obj_soft_grad(h,n,beta): return soft_value_grad(h,n,beta)[1]
def obj_hard(h,n): return hard_value(h,n)
def obj_hard_grad(h,n): return hard_grad(h,n, tol=0.0)[1]
```
For hard, `hard_grad` should return gradient and value? We'll make `hard_grad(h,n)` returns grad only using argmax? SLSQP needs just grad. Could use average active within tol 1e-8*max to smooth. For deterministic, pick one argmax is fine but may oscillate. Use active normalized within 1e-5? Let's implement `obj_hard_grad`:
```
def obj_hard_grad(h,n):
    c=c_full; cmax=np.max(c); active = c >= cmax - 1e-7*max(1.0,cmax)
    if not any: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w=active.astype(float); w/=w.sum()
    return grad_w(w,h,n)*(2.0/n)
```

`active_nlp_refine`:
```
def active_nlp_refine(h,n,stop,rounds=2,active_count=5,maxiter=250):
   best_h=project_h(h,n); best_c=c5(best_h,n)
   best_zc = np.max(c_full(best_h,n))
   for r in range(rounds):
      if time.time() > stop - 10: break
      c = c_full(best_h,n); cmax = float(np.max(c))
      if cmax <= best_zc*(1-1e-10): ...
      top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
      # possibly choose top active_count but ensure all > cmax-... not needed
      cons=[]
      for idx in top[:active_count]:
         cons.append({'type':'ineq','fun':nlp_con_fun,'jac':nlp_con_jac,'args':(n,int(idx))})
      cons.insert(0, {'type':'eq','fun':nlp_sum_fun,'jac':nlp_sum_jac,'args':(n,)})
      z0 = cmax + 1e-8
      x0 = np.empty(n+1); x0[0]=z0; x0[1:]=best_h
      bounds = [(0.0, z0)] + [(0.0,1.0)]*n
      maxiter = 200 if n<150 else 120
      try:
        res = minimize(nlp_obj, x0, jac=nlp_jac, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
        h2 = project_h(res.x[1:], n)
        c2 = c5(h2,n)
        if c2 < best_c: best_h=h2; best_c=c2; best_zc=c2*n/2?.best_zc = max(c_full...)
        else:
           # if res.x z lower but h not? ignore
           if not res.success and (time.time() > stop - 5): break
      except: break
   return best_h,best_c
```
Need `nlp_con_fun` receives x, n, idx:
```
def nlp_con_fun(x,n,idx):
   return float(c_full(x[1:],n)[idx]) - float(x[0])
def nlp_con_jac(x,n,idx):
   g = lag_grad(x[1:],n,idx)
   out = np.empty(len(x)); out[0] = -1.0; out[1:] = g; return out
def nlp_obj(x): return float(x[0])
def nlp_jac(x):
   out=np.zeros(len(x)); out[0]=1.0; return out
def nlp_sum_fun(x,n): return float(np.sum(x[1:])) - 0.5*float(n)
def nlp_sum_jac(x,n):
   out=np.zeros(len(x)); out[1:]=1.0; return out
```
This NLP minimizes zc. Since objective linear, SLSQP may want zc to lower bound 0, constraints prevent. If active constraints not enough, it could reduce z below other c; after solve we check full and add top. Good.
Potential issue: bounds zc lower 0, upper initial cmax. If true minimal feasible z lower than initial, solver can find. Good.

def local_full(h,n,stop):
   h=project; c=c5
   # Phase1 PG: allocate 35% of stop
   p1 = time.time() + (stop-time.time())*0.35
   for beta in [50,300]: h,c = projected_gradient_refine(h,n,beta,stop=p1)
   # Phase2 SLSQP soft: 35%
   p2 = time.time() + (stop-time.time())*0.35
   for beta in [200,800]: h,c = slsqp_soft(h,n,beta,stop=p2)
   # Phase3 hard PG: 10%
   p3 = ...
   h,c=projected_gradient_hard(h,n,stop=p3)
   # Phase4 NLP: 15%
   p4 = ...
   h,c=active_nlp_refine(h,n,stop=p4, active_count=4, rounds=2)
   # Final SLSQP hard if time
   p5 = stop - 10
   h,c=slsqp_hard(h,n,stop=p5)
   # final hard PG tiny
   if time.time()<stop-5: h,c=projected_gradient_hard(h,n,stop)
   return
```

def c_lag_direct(h,n,idx):
 lag=idx-(n-1); m=n-abs(lag)
 if lag>=0:
   d=lag; return dot(1-h[:m], h[d:d+m])
 else:
   d=-lag; return dot(1-h[d:d+m], h[:m])
```

Bisection:
```
def project_h(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=0.5*n
 lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
 hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
   val=sum(clip(h-mid,0,1))
   if val > target: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 h=clip(h- (lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 # fix residual one by one
 diff=target-sum(h)
 for _ in range(10):
  if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
  if diff>0:
    idx=np.argmax(1-h); room=min(diff,1-h[idx]); if room<=0: break; h[idx]+=room; diff-=room
  else:
    idx=np.argmin(h); room=min(-diff,h[idx]); if room<=0: break; h[idx]-=room; diff+=room
 return h
```
This finds λ that clips sum target. Since h already bounded, λ range. But if h has values all 0 or1, clipping with λ may not reach target? Example h all0 target48; lo=-1,hi=1. At mid=0, clip(0,0,1)=0 sum0 <48, hi=0; mid=-0.5, clip(0.5) sum n*0.5=48 for n=96, good. If target >n/2? not. Works. Faster O(50*n) vs iterative maybe similar. Use bisection.
But if h not all 0/1, okay. It shifts all values by λ, not redistributing only non-bound; that's standard projection onto box+sum? Is subtracting constant and clipping the Euclidean projection onto box with sum? Yes, solving ∑ clip(h_i - λ)=target. Good.
Use 60 iterations for precision. Faster than water fill? 60*n operations in C vectorized, okay. Then fix residual with one/two points. Good.
Let's use bisection.

```
def project_h(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n: h=np.resize(h,n)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*float(n)
    s=float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s-target)<1e-14:
       return h
    lo=min(float(np.min(h)),0.0)-1.0
    hi=max(float(np.max(h)),1.0)+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       val=float(np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)))
       if val > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    h=np.clip(h-lam,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
    for _ in range(20):
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          room=min(diff, 1.0-h[idx])
          if room <=0: break
          h[idx]+=room; diff-=room
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(h))
          room=min(-diff, h[idx])
          if room <=0: break
          h[idx]-=room; diff+=room
    return h
```
This is robust. But if target not reachable due round, fine.

```
def projected_gradient_hard(h,n,stop,max_iter=300,temp=0.2):
    h=project_h; best...
    for it ...:
       c=c_full(h); cmax=max
       w=np.exp((c-cmax)/temp); w/=w.sum()
       grad=grad_w(w,h,n)*(2/n); grad-=mean
       gnorm=norm
       if gnorm<1e-13 break
       step=0.05/gnorm
       improved=False
       for _ in range(20):
          h2=project_h(h-step*grad,n)
          c2=float(np.max(c_full(h2))*(2/n))
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
             h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if not improved: break
    return
```
Maybe use `c_full` for trial no dx. Good.

def local_full(h,n,stop):
    best_h=project_h(h,n); best_c=c5
    rem=stop-time.time()
    if rem <= 5: return
    p1=time.time()+0.25*rem

       h2,c2=projected_gradient_refine(best_h,n,beta,stop=p1,max_iter=400)

    return

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf'); best_n=0

    parent_h=None; parent_n=0
    try:
       arr = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if arr.ndim==1 and arr.size>5:
          parent_h=arr; parent_n=arr.size
    except Exception:
       pass

    if parent_h is not None:
       h0=project_h(parent_h,parent_n)
       c0=c5(h0,parent_n)
       best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c0; best_n=parent_n
       hs=project_h(0.5*(h0+h0[::-1]), parent_n)
       cs=c5(hs,parent_n)
       if cs < best_c:
          best_h=hs; best_c=cs; best_n=parent_n; h0=hs
       # if sym not best, keep sym_refine candidate maybe later
       sym_h = hs
    else:
       parent_n=96
       h0=make_unimodal(parent_n,0.5)
       c0=c5(h0,parent_n)
       best_h=h0; best_c=c0; best_n=parent_n
       sym_h=h0

    stage1=t0+min(320.0,0.28*budget_s)
    if time.time()<stage1-20:
       h1,c1,n1=local_full(best_h,best_n,stage1)
       if c1<best_c: update
    # if sym_h different and close, quick
    if parent_h is not None and time.time()<stage1-20:
       hs2,c2=local_quick(sym_h,parent_n,stage1)
       if c2<best_c: update

    stage2=t0+min(700.0,0.55*budget_s)
    # perturbations from best
    if time.time()<stage2-40 and best_n>0:
       scales=[0.08,0.18,0.35]
       for scale in scales:
          if time.time()>stage2-25: break
          hp=project_h(best_h + rng.normal(size=best_n)*scale, best_n)
          hq,cq=local_quick(hp,best_n, min(stage2, time.time()+30))
          if cq<best_c: update
    # unimodal p sweep at n_parent and 96/128?
    n_swaps=[]
    if 80 <= best_n <= 140: n_swaps=[best_n,96]
    else: n_swaps=[96]
    if best_n < 120: n_swaps.append(128)
    # unique
    seen=[]
    for nn in n_swaps: if nn not in seen: seen.append(nn)
    candidates=[]
    for nn in seen:
       if time.time() > stage2-20: break
       for p in [0.25,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0]:

          hi=interp_h(best_h,best_n,nn); candidates.append((c5(hi,nn),hi,nn))
          hs=step_interp(best_h,best_n,nn); candidates.append((c5(hs,nn),hs,nn))
    # sort candidates
    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # lambda not allowed? top-level helper sort? We cannot use lambda in final. Need avoid lambda. Use `candidates = sorted(candidates, key=...)` lambda not allowed? The rule says no closures or lambdas. Yes avoid lambdas.
```

Helper `make_cosine`:
```
def make_cosine(n):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n))
    h=0.5*(1.0-np.cos(np.pi*x))
    return project_h(h,n)
```
`make_unimodal`:
```
def make_unimodal(n,p):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n))
    u=np.abs(x-1.0)
    coeff=0.5*(1.0+float(p))
    h=1.0 - coeff*np.power(u, p)
    return project_h(h,n)
```
For p=2, coeff1.5, at u=1 h=-0.5 clip; area maybe <1 before project; project adds maybe near flat. OK.

`random_smooth_h`:
```
def random_smooth_h(rng,n):
    s=rng.normal(size=n)
    klen=max(3, n//6)
    sig=max(1.0, n/12.0)
    k=np.exp(-np.square(np.arange(-klen,klen+1))/float(2*sig*sig))
    k/=k.sum()
    s=np.convolve(s,k,mode='same')
    s-=s.mean()
    sd=float(np.std(s))
    if sd>1e-12:
       s=np.tanh(0.9*s/sd)
    else:
       s=0.0
    h=0.5+0.5*s
    return project_h(h,n)
```
Sym version externally.

`interp_h`:
```
def interp_h(h,n,new_n):
    if new_n <=0: return h
    if new_n == n: return project_h(h,n)
    xo=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n))
    xn=(np.arange(new_n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(new_n))
    out=np.interp(xn,xo,h,left=h[0],right=h[-1])
    return project_h(out,new_n)
```
`step_interp`:
```
def step_interp(h,n,new_n):
    if new_n==n: return project_h(h,n)
    idx=np.floor(np.arange(new_n)*(float(n)/float(new_n))).astype(int)
    idx=np.clip(idx,0,n-1)
    return project_h(h[idx],new_n)
```

Let's check `obj_hard_grad`: We need return grad for objective `obj_hard` (dx*max). Use active average. Could use `tol` relation in c units. Implement:
```
def obj_hard_grad(h,n):
    c=c_full(h); cmax=float(np.max(c)); scale=max(1.0, abs(cmax))
    active = c >= cmax - 1e-8*scale
    if not np.any(active):
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       w=active.astype(float); w /= float(np.sum(w))
    return grad_w(w,h,n)*(2.0/float(n))
```
For SLSQP, if active many, gradient average. Fine.

`active_nlp_refine`: Need after each round update `best_zc` maybe not; not needed. We'll just run rounds from current best_h.
```
def active_nlp_refine(h,n,stop,rounds=2,active_count=4):
    best_h=project_h(h,n); best_c=c5(best_h,n)
    for r in range(rounds):
       if time.time() > stop - 10: break
       c=c_full(best_h); cmax=float(np.max(c))
       top=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       active_idx=[int(top[i]) for i in range(min(active_count,len(top)))]
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':nlp_sum_fun,'jac':nlp_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
       for idx in active_idx:
          cons.append({'type':'ineq','fun':nlp_con_fun,'jac':nlp_con_jac,'args':(n,idx)})
       z0=cmax+1e-8
       x0=np.empty(n+1); x0[0]=z0; x0[1:]=best_h
       bounds=[(0.0,z0)] + [(0.0,1.0)]*n
       maxiter=180 if n<120 else 100
       try:
           res=minimize(nlp_obj,x0,jac=nlp_jac,constraints=cons,bounds=bounds,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
           h2=project_h(res.x[1:],n)
           c2=c5(h2,n)
           if c2 < best_c:
               best_h=h2; best_c=c2
           # optionally if no improve break
           if c2 >= best_c - 1e-14:
               # if solver didn't help, maybe break to save
               if r > 0: break
       except Exception:
           break
    return best_h,best_c
```
But after first round if no improve, break; if improve, second round. Good.

def nlp_con_fun(x,n,idx):
    h=x[1:]
    return float(c_lag_value(h,n,idx)) - float(x[0])
```

`c_lag_value`:
```
def c_lag_value(h,n,idx):
    lag=idx-(n-1)
    if lag>=0:
       d=lag; m=n-d
       if m<=0: return 0.0
       return float(np.dot(1.0-h[:m], h[d:d+m]))
    else:
       d=-lag; m=n-d
       if m<=0: return 0.0
       return float(np.dot(1.0-h[d:d+m], h[:m]))
```
For d=0, positive branch dot(q,h) correct.
`lag_grad` matches.

Potential orientation of c_full? If c_full uses `np.correlate(h,1-h)`, and direct says positive lag d: dot(1-h[:m], h[d:]). Let's test with n=3 h=[a,b,c]. direct lag index? idx=3? n-1=2, lag1: c = q0*h1 + q1*h2 = A b + B c. Previous negative? This is convolve orientation we derived from previous gradient. We believe `np.correlate(h,q)` positive lag (idx n-1+1=3) =? If formula c_t=∑ q_i h_{t-i}, t=3,n=3 => i=0:q0 h3 invalid? Wait t index 0..4? For n=3, full length5, idx=3 corresponds t=3. c_t=∑ q_i h_{t-i}, valid t-i 0..2 => i=t-2..t. For t=3: i=1,2? q1 h2 + q2 h1. Not q0h1+q1h2. Hmm confusion. The indexing offset may be different.
Let's compute convolution `c = convolve(q, flip(h))`. q length n, flip(h) length n. Full length 2n-1, index t from0 to2n-2. c_t=∑_{i=max(0,t-(n-1))}^{min(n-1,t)} q_i * flip(h)_{t-i} = q_i * h_{n-1-(t-i)} = q_i h_{n-1-t+i}. Let k = n-1-t. Then c_t=∑ q_i h_{i+k}. For t=3,n=3: k=2-3=-1, c=∑ q_i h_{i-1}, i=1,2 => q1 h0 + q2 h1. Not previous. For positive lag k=1 (t=1): k=2-1=1, i=0,1 => q0 h1 + q1 h2. So index for positive lag k=1 is t=1, not 3. The `c` array order is decreasing lag: idx=0 lag n-1? Our mapping in previous code `lag=idx-(n-1)`: idx=1 => lag=-1. Wait previous code maps idx n-1+lag. If c_t with k=n-1-t, then lag = -k. Previous positive lag d corresponds idx = n-1-d. Let's check: If we want c=∑ q_i h_{i+d} (positive d), t= n-1-d, idx t. Then previous `lag = idx - (n-1) = -d`. But previous code for lag>=0 used c=∑ q_i h_{i+lag}? There is a sign inconsistency.
Let's revisit previous code: It set lag=idx-(n-1). If lag>=0, used gradient for c=∑ q_i h_{i+lag}? Or derived? We earlier matched previous positive code to c=∑ q_i h_{i+lag}. But if np positive lag in our conv coordinate is actually negative in index. However because max all, the set of constraints indexed by lag in code may be swapped: For idx with previous lag positive, actual c may be negative lag. ```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-14:
        return h

    lo = min(float(np.min(h)), 0.0) - 1.0
    hi = max(float(np.max(h)), 1.0) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    h = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    for _ in range(20):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            if room <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += room
            diff -= room
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            room = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if room <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] -= room
            diff += room
    return h


def c_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(c_full(h)) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def make_unimodal(n, p):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    u = np.abs(x - 1.0)
    coeff = 0.5 * (1.0 + float(p))
    h = 1.0 - coeff * np.power(u, float(p))
    return project_h(h, n)


def make_cosine(n):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.cos(np.pi * x))
    return project_h(h, n)


def random_smooth_h(rng, n):
    s = rng.normal(size=n)
    klen = max(3, n // 6)
    sig = max(1.0, n / 12.0)
    k = np.exp(-np.square(np.arange(-klen, klen + 1)) / float(2.0 * sig * sig))
    k /= k.sum()
    s = np.convolve(s, k, mode="same")
    s = s - float(np.mean(s))
    sd = float(np.std(s))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        s = np.tanh(0.9 * s / sd)
    else:
        s = 0.0
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * s
    return project_h(h, n)


def random_blocks_h(rng, n):
    h = project_h((rng.random(n) > 0.5).astype(float), n)
    return h


def interp_h(h, n, new_n):
    if new_n <= 0:
        return h
    if new_n == n:
        return project_h(h, n)
    xo = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    xn = (np.arange(new_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(new_n))
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_h(out, new_n)


def step_interp(h, n, new_n):
    if new_n <= 0:
        return h
    if new_n == n:
        return project_h(h, n)
    idx = np.floor(np.arange(new_n) * (float(n) / float(new_n))).astype(int)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n - 1)
    return project_h(h[idx], new_n)


def grad_w(weights, h, n):
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    for idx in range(w.size):
        wk = float(w[idx])
        if wk <= 1e-16:
            continue
        lag = idx - (n - 1)
        if lag >= 0:
            m = n - lag
            grad[lag:] += wk * (1.0 - h[:m])
            if m > 0:
                grad[:m] -= wk * h[lag:]
        else:
            d = -lag
            m = n - d
            if m > 0:
                grad[:m] += wk * (1.0 - h[d:])
                if d < n:
                    grad[d:] -= wk * h[:m]
    return grad


def c_lag_value(h, n, idx):
    lag = idx - (n - 1)
    if lag >= 0:
        d = lag
        if d < 0 or d >= n:
            return 0.0
        m = n - d
        return float(np.dot(1.0 - h[:m], h[d:d + m]))
    else:
        d = -lag
        if d < 0 or d >= n:
            return 0.0
        m = n - d
        return float(np.dot(1.0 - h[d:d + m], h[:m]))


def lag_grad(h, n, idx):
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    lag = idx - (n - 1)
    if lag >= 0:
        d = lag
        m = n - d
        grad[d:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
        if m > 0:
            grad[:m] -= h[d:]
    else:
        d = -lag
        m = n - d
        if m > 0:
            grad[:m] += 1.0 - h[d:]
            if d < n:
                grad[d:] -= h[:m]
    return grad


def soft_value(h, n, beta):
    c = c_full(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    s = beta * c
    m = float(np.max(s))
    e = np.exp(s - m)
    z = float(np.sum(e))
    if z <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float((m + np.log(z)) * dx / float(beta))


def soft_value_grad(h, n, beta):
    c = c_full(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    s = beta * c
    m = float(np.max(s))
    e = np.exp(s - m)
    z = float(np.sum(e))
    if z <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    weights = e / z
    val = float((m + np.log(z)) * dx / float(beta))
    grad = grad_w(weights, h, n) * dx
    return val, grad


def obj_soft(h, n, beta):
    return soft_value(h, n, beta)


def obj_soft_grad(h, n, beta):
    return soft_value_grad(h, n, beta)[1]


def obj_hard(h, n):
    return c5(h, n)


def obj_hard_grad(h, n):
    c = c_full(h)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    scale = max(1.0, abs(cmax))
    active = c >= cmax - 1e-8 * scale
    if not np.any(active):
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = active.astype(float)
        w /= float(np.sum(w))
    return grad_w(w, h, n) * (2.0 / float(n))


def sum_eq_fun(h, n):
    return float(np.sum(h)) - 0.5 * float(n)


def sum_eq_jac(h, n):
    return np.ones(len(h), dtype=float)


def nlp_obj(x):
    return float(x[0])


def nlp_jac(x):
    out = np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    out[0] = 1.0
    return out


def nlp_sum_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x[1:])) - 0.5 * float(n)


def nlp_sum_jac(x, n):
    out = np.zeros(len(x), dtype=float)
    out[1:] = 1.0
    return out


def nlp_con_fun(x, n, idx):
    return float(x[0]) - c_lag_value(x[1:], n, idx)


def nlp_con_jac(x, n, idx):
    g = lag_grad(x[1:], n, idx)
    out = np.empty(len(x), dtype=float)
    out[0] = 1.0
    out[1:] = -g
    return out


def projected_gradient_refine(h, n, beta, stop, max_iter=500, lr0=0.05):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)

    F, grad = soft_value_grad(h, n, beta)
    alpha = lr0
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > stop:
            break

        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        step = min(alpha, 0.2 / max(gnorm, 1e-14))
        old_F = F
        F2 = F + 1.0
        improved = False

        for _back in range(25):
            h2 = project_h(h - step * grad, n)
            F2 = soft_value(h2, n, beta)
            if F2 < F - 1e-15:
                h = h2
                F = F2
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            for tiny in (0.01 / max(gnorm, 1e-14), 0.004 / max(gnorm, 1e-14), 0.0015 / max(gnorm, 1e-14)):
                h2 = project_h(h - tiny * grad, n)
                c2 = c5(h2, n)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                    h = h2
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    break
            break

        c2 = c5(h, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                break

        if F2 < old_F - 1e-12:
            alpha = min(0.5, alpha * 1.1)
        else:
            alpha *= 0.75

    best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
    best_c = min(best_c, c5(best_h, n))
    return best_h, best_c


def projected_gradient_hard(h, n, stop, max_iter=500, temp=0.15):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > stop:
            break

        c = c_full(h)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        w = np.exp((c - cmax) / temp)
        som = float(np.sum(w))
        if som <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=float)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / som

        grad = grad_w(w, h, n) * (2.0 / float(n))
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-13:
            break

        step = 0.05 / max(gnorm, 1e-14)
        improved = False
        for _back in range(20):
            h2 = project_h(h - step * grad, n)
            c2 = float(np.max(c_full(h2)) * (2.0 / float(n)))
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                h = h2
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h.copy()
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            break

    best_h = project_h(best_h, n)
    best_c = min(best_c, c5(best_h, n))
    return best_h, best_c


def _slsqp_maxiter(n, base=350):
    if n <= 110:
        return int(base * 1.4)
    if n <= 190:
        return int(base)
    return int(base * 0.6)


def slsqp_soft(h, n, beta, stop):
    best_h = project_h(h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)
    if time.time() > stop - 25.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_eq_fun, 'jac': sum_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun=obj_soft,
            x0=best_h,
            jac=obj_soft_grad,
            args=(n, beta),
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': _slsqp_maxiter(n, 330), 'ftol': 1e-13}
        )
        h2 = project_h(res.x, n)
        c2 = c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            return h2, c2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c


def slsqp_hard(h, n, stop):
    best_h = project_h(h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)
    if time.time() > stop - 25.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_eq_fun, 'jac': sum_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun=obj_hard,
            x0=best_h,
            jac=obj_hard_grad,
            args=(n,),
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': _slsqp_maxiter(n, 280), 'ftol': 1e-14}
        )
        h2 = project_h(res.x, n)
        c2 = c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            return h2, c2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c


def active_nlp_refine(h, n, stop, rounds=2, active_count=4):
    best_h = project_h(h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)

    for r in range(rounds):
        if time.time() > stop - 12.0:
            break

        c = c_full(best_h)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        idxs = [int(top[i]) for i in range(min(active_count, len(top)))]

        cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': nlp_sum_fun, 'jac': nlp_sum_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
        for idx in idxs:
            cons.append({'type': 'ineq', 'fun': nlp_con_fun, 'jac': nlp_con_jac, 'args': (n, idx)})

        z0 = cmax + 1e-8
        x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
        x0[0] = z0
        x0[1:] = best_h

        bounds = [(0.0, z0)] + [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        maxiter = 160 if n <= 110 else (110 if n <= 190 else 80)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                nlp_obj,
                x0,
                jac=nlp_jac,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12}
            )
            h2 = project_h(res.x[1:], n)
            c2 = c5(h2, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
            else:
                if r > 0:
                    break
        except Exception:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def local_quick(h, n, stop):
    best_h = project_h(h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)
    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem <= 5.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    p1 = time.time() + 0.65 * rem
    for beta in (120.0, 500.0):
        if time.time() > p1:
            break
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient_refine(best_h, n, beta, stop=p1, max_iter=260)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if time.time() < stop - 5.0:
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient_hard(best_h, n, stop=stop, max_iter=260, temp=0.22)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    return best_h, best_c


def local_full(h, n, stop):
    best_h = project_h(h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)
    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem <= 8.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    p1 = time.time() + 0.25 * rem
    for beta in (80.0, 450.0):
        if time.time() > p1:
            break
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient_refine(best_h, n, beta, stop=p1, max_iter=420)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem > 25.0:
        p2 = time.time() + 0.45 * rem
        for beta in (250.0, 1200.0):
            if time.time() > p2:
                break
            h2, c2 = slsqp_soft(best_h, n, beta, stop=p2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2

    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem > 12.0:
        p3 = time.time() + 0.16 * rem
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient_hard(best_h, n, stop=p3, max_iter=360, temp=0.1)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem > 25.0:
        p4 = time.time() + 0.24 * rem
        h2, c2 = active_nlp_refine(best_h, n, stop=p4, rounds=2, active_count=4)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    rem = max(0.0, stop - time.time())
    if rem > 25.0:
        h2, c2 = slsqp_hard(best_h, n, stop=stop)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

        if time.time() < stop - 6.0:
            h2, c2 = projected_gradient_hard(best_h, n, stop=stop, max_iter=140, temp=0.06)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2

    return project_h(best_h, n), min(best_c, c5(best_h, n))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = 0

    parent_h = None
    parent_n = 0
    try:
        arr = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 5:
            parent_h = arr
            parent_n = int(arr.size)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None
        parent_n = 0

    if parent_h is not None and parent_n > 0:
        h0 = project_h(parent_h, parent_n)
        c0 = c5(h0, parent_n)
        best_h = h0
        best_c = c0
        best_n = parent_n

        hs = project_h(0.5 * (h0 + h0[::-1]), parent_n)
        cs = c5(hs, parent_n)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h = hs
            best_c = cs
            h0 = hs
    else:
        parent_n = 96
        h0 = make_unimodal(parent_n, 0.5)
        c0 = c5(h0, parent_n)
        best_h = h0
        best_c = c0
        best_n = parent_n

    if best_h is None:
        parent_n = 96
        best_h = make_unimodal(parent_n, 0.5)
        best_c = c5(best_h, parent_n)
        best_n = parent_n

    stage1 = t0 + min(340.0, 0.30 * float(budget_s))
    if time.time() < stage1 - 25.0:
        h1, c1, n1 = local_full(best_h, best_n, stage1)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, n1

    if parent_h is not None and time.time() < stage1 - 20.0:
        hs2 = project_h(0.5 * (parent_h + parent_h[::-1]), parent_n)
        h2, c2 = local_quick(hs2, parent_n, stage1)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, parent_n

    stage2 = t0 + min(720.0, 0.55 * float(budget_s))

    if time.time() < stage2 - 50.0 and best_n > 0:
        for scale in (0.09, 0.20, 0.38):
            if time.time() > stage2 - 30.0:
                break
            hp = project_h(best_h + rng.normal(size=best_n) * scale, best_n)
            hq, cq = local_quick(hp, best_n, min(stage2, time.time() + 32.0))
            if cq < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, best_n

    candidates = []
    cand_id = 0

    swap_ns = []
    if 80 <= best_n <= 145:
        swap_ns.append(best_n)
    if 96 not in swap_ns:
        swap_ns.append(96)
    if best_n < 128:
        swap_ns.append(128)

    for nn in swap_ns:
        if time.time() > stage2 - 30.0:
            break
        for p in (0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
            hh = make_unimodal(nn, p)
            cc = c5(hh, nn)
            candidates.append((cc, cand_id, hh, nn))
            cand_id += 1

        hc = make_cosine(nn)
        cc = c5(hc, nn)
        candidates.append((cc, cand_id, hc, nn))
        cand_id += 1

        if best_h is not None:
            hi = interp_h(best_h, best_n, nn)
            ci = c5(hi, nn)
            candidates.append((ci, cand_id, hi, nn))
            cand_id += 1

            hs = step_interp(best_h, best_n, nn)
            cstep = c5(hs, nn)
            candidates.append((cstep, cand_id, hs, nn))
            cand_id += 1

    candidates.sort()
    top_limit = 0
    for item in candidates:
        if top_limit >= 3:
            break
        if time.time() > stage2 - 45.0:
            break
        alloc = min(65.0, max(12.0, (stage2 - time.time()) / 2.0))
        hh, nn = item[2], item[3]
        hq, cq = local_quick(hh, nn, time.time() + alloc)
        if cq < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, nn
        top_limit += 1

    if candidates and time.time() < stage2 - 90.0:
        item = None
        for it in candidates:
            if it[0] < best_c + 0.02:
                item = it
                break
        if item is not None:
            hq, cq = local_full(item[2], item[3], stage2)
            if cq < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, item[3]

    stage3 = t0 + min(1020.0, 0.78 * float(budget_s))
    rand_ns = []
    if 60 <= best_n <= 135:
        rand_ns.append(best_n)
    if 96 not in rand_ns:
        rand_ns.append(96)
    if 128 not in rand_ns:
        rand_ns.append(128)

    uniq_rand = []
    for nn in rand_ns:
        if nn > 40 and nn not in uniq_rand:
            uniq_rand.append(nn)
    rand_ns = uniq_rand[:2]

    for nn in rand_ns:
        count = 7
        for i in range(count):
            if time.time() > stage3 - 35.0:
                break
            if i % 3 == 0:
                hr = random_blocks_h(rng, nn)
            else:
                hr = random_smooth_h(rng, nn)
            hr = project_h(0.5 * (hr + hr[::-1]), nn)
            alloc = min(35.0, max(10.0, (stage3 - time.time()) / float(max(1, count - i))))
            hq, cq = local_quick(hr, nn, time.time() + alloc)
            if cq < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, nn

    stage4 = t0 + min(1230.0, 0.92 * float(budget_s))
    hi_ns_raw = [144, 176, 192, 224, 256, 304]
    hi_ns = []
    for nn in hi_ns_raw:
        if best_n > 0 and nn > best_n and nn <= 320 and nn not in hi_ns:
            hi_ns.append(nn)
    if not hi_ns and best_n < 192:
        hi_ns = [192]

    for nn in hi_ns[:2]:
        if time.time() > stage4 - 80.0:
            break
        hi = interp_h(best_h, best_n, nn)
        hq, cq = local_quick(hi, nn, min(stage4, time.time() + 130.0))
        if cq < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, nn
        if cq < best_c - 1e-6 and time.time() < stage4 - 70.0:
            h2, c2 = local_full(best_h, best_n, stage4)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, best_n

    final_stop = t0 + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 18.0)
    if time.time() < final_stop - 25.0:
        h2, c2, n2 = local_full(best_h, best_n, final_stop)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, n2

    best_h = project_h(best_h, int(best_n))
    best_c = min(best_c, c5(best_h, best_n))

    eval_func = globals().get('evaluate_erdos_solution', None)
    if callable(eval_func):
        try:
            val = eval_func(best_h, int(best_n))
            if val is not None:
                best_c = min(best_c, float(val))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return (best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n))
```
